1a:["$","p",null,{"children":["A list changes through ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::push(item)"}],", which moves the item in, and through\nits items; both need a binding that allows changes (",["$","code",null,{"children":"mut"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"open"}],", or a\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"&mut"}]," borrow), or the compiler says so (E0205, E0311). 1c:["$","section",null,{"className":"drh-step","children":[["$","div",null,{"className":"drh-prose","children":[["$","$L16",null,{"as":"h2","id":"reading-and-removing-items","children":"Reading and removing items"}],["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::pop()"}]," takes the last item out, and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::remove(i)"}]," takes the item at\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"i"}]," out (the items after it move down). Both give the item, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"pop"}]," as a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"T?"}],"\nthat is ",["$","code",null,{"children":"none"}]," for an empty list; ",["$","code",null,{"children":"remove"}]," stops the program when ",["$","code",null,{"children":"i"}]," is\noutside the list. ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::insert(i, item)"}]," puts an item in at ",["$","code",null,{"children":"i"}]," (",["$","code",null,{"children":"i"}]," may be\nthe length, to add at the end). ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::clear()"}]," drops every item and keeps the\nlist usable."]}],["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::get(i)"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::first()"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::last()"}]," give the item as a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"T?"}],", which is\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"none"}]," when there is no such item, so they never stop the program. They copy\nthe item, so for items that copy (numbers, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"bool"}],", Copy structs) that is all\nthere is to it. For items that own memory (",["$","code",null,{"children":"string"}],", structs with a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"string"}],")\nthe answer is a view of the item: an optional that holds a borrow of the list\n(written ",["$","code",null,{"children":"maybe &string"}],"). While the\nview is in use the list cannot change (",["$","code",null,{"children":"push"}]," is E0302), exactly as for\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"&xs[0]"}],". ",["$","code",null,{"children":"copy xs::first()"}]," gives an owned ",["$","code",null,{"children":"T?"}]," copy of the item instead, and\na view cannot be assigned to an owned ",["$","code",null,{"children":"string?"}]," (E0531, which points at\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"copy"}],"). Read a view with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"maybe &fallback"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"has value"}],", or ",["$","code",null,{"children":"match"}]," with a\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"&T"}]," pattern: ",["$","code",null,{"children":"case &string n { ... }"}],". ",["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::join(sep)"}]," puts the items\nof a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"string[?]"}]," together with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"sep"}]," between them (",["$","code",null,{"children":"\"\""}]," for an empty list)."]}],["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"xs::contains(item)"}]," is true when some item is equal to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"item"}],".
